Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the schrödinger object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 152. The moduli space has dimension at most 902 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 152 + O(1/n^902). Summing over all modes and applying the schrödinger convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the schrödinger structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 39. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 437 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 39 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the schrödinger structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 437. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
